=== FILE: \src\components\editor\analytics\AnalyticsContainer.vue ===
<script setup>
import DateFilter from './dateFilter.vue'
import { ref, computed } from 'vue'

const props = defineProps({
  tasks: Array,
  manuscripts: Array,
  reviewers: Array,
  redactors: Array,
})

const dateFilter = ref({ type: 'month' })

const handleDateChange = (filter) => {
  dateFilter.value = filter
}

/* Р Р°СЃС‡С‘С‚ РґР°С‚ РґР»СЏ С„РёР»СЊС‚СЂР° */
const filteredManuscripts = computed(() => {
  const now = new Date()

  return props.manuscripts.filter((m) => {
    const date = new Date(m.sentDate)

    if (dateFilter.value.type === 'month') {
      const monthAgo = new Date()
      monthAgo.setMonth(now.getMonth() - 1)
      return date >= monthAgo
    }

    if (dateFilter.value.type === '3months') {
      const threeMonthsAgo = new Date()
      threeMonthsAgo.setMonth(now.getMonth() - 3)
      return date >= threeMonthsAgo
    }

    if (dateFilter.value.type === 'year') {
      const yearAgo = new Date()
      yearAgo.setFullYear(now.getFullYear() - 1)
      return date >= yearAgo
    }

    if (dateFilter.value.type === 'custom') {
      const from = new Date(dateFilter.value.from)
      const to = new Date(dateFilter.value.to)
      return date >= from && date <= to
    }

    return true
  })
})

const myManuscriptsCount = computed(
  () => filteredManuscripts.value.filter((m) => m.redactorsId?.includes(1)).length,
)

const inProgressCount = computed(
  () => filteredManuscripts.value.filter((m) => m.stage === 'Р’ РїСЂРѕС†РµСЃСЃРµ').length,
)

const declinedCount = computed(
  () => filteredManuscripts.value.filter((m) => m.stage === 'РћС‚РєР»РѕРЅРµРЅРѕ').length,
)

const publishedCount = computed(
  () => filteredManuscripts.value.filter((m) => m.stage === 'РћРїСѓР±Р»РёРєРѕРІР°РЅРѕ').length,
)
</script>

<template>
  <div>
    <div class="section-name">РђРЅР°Р»РёС‚РёРєР° РїСЂРѕРґСѓРєС‚РёРІРЅРѕСЃС‚Рё</div>

    <DateFilter @change="handleDateChange" />

    <div class="analytics-container">
      <div class="stat-card">
        <div class="stat-title">Р’СЃРµ СЂСѓРєРѕРїРёСЃРё</div>
        <div class="stat-value">{{ myManuscriptsCount }}</div>
      </div>

      <div class="stat-card">
        <div class="stat-title">РќР° СЂР°СЃСЃРјРѕС‚СЂРµРЅРёРё</div>
        <div class="stat-value">{{ inProgressCount }}</div>
      </div>

      <div class="stat-card">
        <div class="stat-title">РћС‚РєР»РѕРЅС‘РЅРЅС‹Рµ</div>
        <div class="stat-value">{{ declinedCount }}</div>
      </div>

      <div class="stat-card">
        <div class="stat-title">РћРїСѓР±Р»РёРєРѕРІР°РЅРЅС‹Рµ</div>
        <div class="stat-value">{{ publishedCount }}</div>
      </div>
    </div>
  </div>
</template>

=== FILE: \src\components\editor\analytics\dateFilter.vue ===
<script setup>
import { ref } from 'vue'
import VueFeather from 'vue-feather'

const emit = defineEmits(['change'])

const isOpen = ref(false)
const selected = ref('month')

const customFrom = ref('')
const customTo = ref('')

const options = [
  { label: 'РџРѕСЃР»РµРґРЅРёР№ РјРµСЃСЏС†', value: 'month' },
  { label: 'РџРѕСЃР»РµРґРЅРёРµ 3 РјРµСЃСЏС†Р°', value: '3months' },
  { label: 'РџРѕСЃР»РµРґРЅРёР№ РіРѕРґ', value: 'year' },
  { label: 'Р’С‹Р±СЂР°С‚СЊ РїРµСЂРёРѕРґ', value: 'custom' },
]

const selectOption = (value) => {
  selected.value = value

  if (value !== 'custom') {
    emit('change', { type: value })
    isOpen.value = false
  }
}

const applyCustom = () => {
  emit('change', {
    type: 'custom',
    from: customFrom.value,
    to: customTo.value,
  })
  isOpen.value = false
}
</script>

<template>
  <div class="sort-wrapper">
    <div class="sort-btn" @click="isOpen = !isOpen" style="margin-top: var(--spacing-md)">
      Р¤РёР»СЊС‚СЂ РїРѕ РґР°С‚Рµ
      <vue-feather
        type="chevron-down"
        size="20"
        :style="{ transform: isOpen ? 'rotate(180deg)' : 'rotate(0)' }"
      />
    </div>

    <div v-if="isOpen" class="dropdown">
      <div v-for="opt in options" :key="opt.value" class="option" @click="selectOption(opt.value)">
        <input type="radio" :checked="selected === opt.value" />
        {{ opt.label }}
      </div>

      <!-- РєР°СЃС‚РѕРјРЅС‹Р№ РїРµСЂРёРѕРґ -->
      <div v-if="selected === 'custom'" style="margin-top: 10px">
        <input type="date" v-model="customFrom" class="input" />
        <input type="date" v-model="customTo" class="input" style="margin-top: 6px" />

        <div class="bright-button editor" @click="applyCustom">РџСЂРёРјРµРЅРёС‚СЊ</div>
      </div>
    </div>
  </div>
</template>

=== FILE: \src\components\editor\manuscriptDetails\AddManuscriptFileButton.vue ===
<script setup>
import axios from 'axios'

const props = defineProps({
  manuscript: Object,
})

const handleFileChange = async (event) => {
  const file = event.target.files[0]

  if (!file) return

  const newPath = `./public/manuscripts/${file.name}`

  const files = [...props.manuscript.manuscriptFiles, newPath]

  await axios.patch(`http://localhost:3001/manuscripts/${props.manuscript.id}`, {
    manuscriptFiles: files,
    version: props.manuscript.version + 1,
  })

  location.reload()
}
</script>

<template>
  <label class="general-button editor">
    Р”РѕР±Р°РІРёС‚СЊ С„Р°Р№Р»
    <input type="file" style="display: none" @change="handleFileChange" />
  </label>
</template>

=== FILE: \src\components\editor\manuscriptDetails\ManuscriptDetailsContainer.vue ===
<script setup>
import { computed, ref, watch } from 'vue'
import axios from 'axios'

import DeleteFileButton from './DeleteFileButton.vue'
import ReplaceFileButton from './ReplaceFileButton.vue'
import AddManuscriptFileButton from './AddManuscriptFileButton.vue'
import ChangeRedactors from './ChangeRedactors.vue'

const showDeleteOverlay = ref(false)
const fileIndexToDelete = ref(null)

const showRedactorsOverlay = ref(false)

const selectedRedactors = ref([])
const searchQuery = ref('')

const showActionModal = ref(false)
const actionType = ref(null)
const messageText = ref('')

const releases = ref([])
const volumes = ref([])
const selectedVolume = ref(null)

const props = defineProps({
  manuscript: Object,
  authors: Array,
  redactors: Array,
  reviews: Array,
  reviewers: Array,
})

watch(
  () => props.manuscript,
  () => {
    selectedRedactors.value = [...props.manuscript.redactorsId]
  },
  { immediate: true },
)

/* РђРІС‚РѕСЂС‹ СЂСѓРєРѕРїРёСЃРё */
const manuscriptAuthors = computed(() => {
  return props.manuscript.authorsId.map((id) => props.authors.find((a) => a.authorsId === id))
})

/* Р РµРґР°РєС‚РѕСЂС‹ */
const manuscriptRedactors = computed(() => {
  return props.redactors.filter((r) => props.manuscript.redactorsId.includes(r.redactorsId))
})

/* Р РµС†РµРЅР·РёРё */
const manuscriptReviews = computed(() => {
  return props.reviews.filter((r) => Number(r.manuscriptId) === Number(props.manuscript.id))
})

/* Р РµС†РµРЅР·РёРё СЃРІСЏР·С‹РІР°РµРј СЃ СЂРµС†РµРЅР·РµРЅС‚Р°РјРё */
const reviewsWithReviewer = computed(() => {
  return manuscriptReviews.value.map((review) => {
    const reviewer = props.reviewers.find((r) => r.reviewersId === review.reviewersId)

    return {
      ...review,
      reviewerName: reviewer?.name || 'РќРµРёР·РІРµСЃС‚РЅС‹Р№ СЂРµС†РµРЅР·РµРЅС‚',
    }
  })
})

/* РџРѕРёСЃРє СЂРµРґР°РєС‚РѕСЂРѕРІ */
const filteredRedactors = computed(() => {
  if (!searchQuery.value) return props.redactors

  return props.redactors.filter(
    (r) =>
      r.name.toLowerCase().includes(searchQuery.value.toLowerCase()) ||
      r.mail.toLowerCase().includes(searchQuery.value.toLowerCase()),
  )
})

/* РЈРґР°Р»РµРЅРёРµ С„Р°Р№Р»Р° */
const deleteFile = async () => {
  const files = [...props.manuscript.manuscriptFiles]

  files.splice(fileIndexToDelete.value, 1)

  await axios.patch(`http://localhost:3001/manuscripts/${props.manuscript.manuscriptId}`, {
    manuscriptFiles: files,
    version: props.manuscript.version + 1,
  })

  location.reload()
}

/* РћС‚РєСЂС‹С‚РёРµ РѕРІРµСЂР»РµСЏ СѓРІРµСЂРµРЅРЅРѕСЃС‚Рё */
const openDeleteOverlay = (index) => {
  fileIndexToDelete.value = index
  showDeleteOverlay.value = true
}

/* РЎРѕС…СЂР°РЅРёС‚СЊ СЂРµРґР°РєС‚РѕСЂРѕРІ */
const saveRedactors = async () => {
  const newEvent = {
    event: 'РЎРїРёСЃРѕРє СЂРµРґР°РєС‚РѕСЂРѕРІ РёР·РјРµРЅС‘РЅ',
    user: 1,
    date: new Date().toISOString().split('T')[0],
  }

  await axios.patch(`http://localhost:3001/manuscripts/${props.manuscript.id}`, {
    redactorsId: selectedRedactors.value,
    events: [...(props.manuscript.events || []), newEvent],
  })

  location.reload()
}

/*Р”РµР№СЃС‚РІРёСЏ СЃ СЂСѓРєРѕРїРёСЃСЊСЋ*/
const actionTitleMap = {
  reject: 'РћС‚РєР»РѕРЅРёС‚СЊ',
  revision: 'РќР° РґРѕСЂР°Р±РѕС‚РєСѓ',
  publish: 'РћРїСѓР±Р»РёРєРѕРІР°С‚СЊ',
}

const stageMap = {
  reject: 'РћС‚РєР»РѕРЅРµРЅРѕ',
  revision: 'РќР° РґРѕСЂР°Р±РѕС‚РєРµ',
  publish: 'РћРїСѓР±Р»РёРєРѕРІР°РЅРѕ',
}

const notifTitleMap = {
  reject: 'Р СѓРєРѕРїРёСЃСЊ РѕС‚РєР»РѕРЅРµРЅР°',
  revision: 'Р СѓРєРѕРїРёСЃСЊ РѕС‚РїСЂР°РІР»РµРЅР° РЅР° РґРѕСЂР°Р±РѕС‚РєСѓ',
  publish: 'Р СѓРєРѕРїРёСЃСЊ РѕРїСѓР±Р»РёРєРѕРІР°РЅР°',
}

/*РџРѕРґРіСЂСѓР¶Р°РµРј С‚РµРєСѓС‰РёР№ РІС‹РїСѓСЃРє Рё С‚РѕРјР° РІС‹РїСѓСЃРєР°*/
const loadPublishingData = async () => {
  const [relRes, volRes] = await Promise.all([
    axios.get('http://localhost:3001/releases'),
    axios.get('http://localhost:3001/volumes'),
  ])

  releases.value = relRes.data
  volumes.value = volRes.data
}

const currentRelease = computed(() => {
  const currentYear = new Date().getFullYear()
  return releases.value.find((r) => r.year === currentYear)
})

const currentVolumes = computed(() => {
  if (!currentRelease.value) return []

  return volumes.value.filter((v) => currentRelease.value.volumes.includes(v.volId))
})

/*РћС‚РєСЂС‹С‚РёРµ РѕРєРЅР° РїРѕСЃР»Рµ РЅР°Р¶Р°С‚РёСЏ РЅР° РєРЅРѕРїРєРё СѓРґР°Р»РёС‚СЊ/РѕС‚РїСЂР°РІРёС‚СЊ РЅР° РґРѕСЂР°Р±РѕС‚РєСѓ/РѕРїСѓР±Р»РёРєРѕРІР°С‚СЊ СЂСѓРєРѕРїРёСЃСЊ */
const openActionModal = async (type) => {
  actionType.value = type
  messageText.value = ''
  showActionModal.value = true

  if (type === 'publish') {
    await loadPublishingData()
  }
}

/*РЎРѕРІРµСЂС€РµРЅРёРµ РґРµР№СЃС‚РІРёСЏ СЃ СЂСѓРєРѕРїРёСЃСЊСЋ*/
const submitAction = async () => {
  const today = new Date().toISOString().split('T')[0]

  /* 1. РѕР±РЅРѕРІР»СЏРµРј СЃРѕР±С‹С‚РёСЏ СЂСѓРєРѕРїРёСЃРё */
  const updatedEvents = [
    ...(props.manuscript.events || []),
    {
      event: notifTitleMap[actionType.value],
      user: 1,
      date: today,
    },
  ]

  await axios.patch(`http://localhost:3001/manuscripts/${props.manuscript.id}`, {
    stage: stageMap[actionType.value],
    events: updatedEvents,
  })

  if (actionType.value === 'reject') {
    await deleteTaskByManuscript()
  }

  if (actionType.value === 'publish') {
    await deleteTaskByManuscript()
  }

  // 2. РїРѕР»СѓС‡Р°РµРј РїРѕР»СЊР·РѕРІР°С‚РµР»РµР№
  const usersRes = await axios.get('http://localhost:3001/users')
  const users = usersRes.data

  // 3. РёС‰РµРј Р°РІС‚РѕСЂРѕРІ в†’ РїРѕР»СЊР·РѕРІР°С‚РµР»РµР№
  const authors = props.manuscript.authorsId

  const targetUsers = users.filter((u) => authors.includes(u.userId))

  // 4. РґРѕР±Р°РІР»СЏРµРј СѓРІРµРґРѕРјР»РµРЅРёСЏ
  for (const user of targetUsers) {
    const newNotif = {
      id: Date.now(),
      title: notifTitleMap[actionType.value],
      text: messageText.value,
      date: today,
    }

    const updatedNotifs = [...(user.notifs || []), newNotif]

    await axios.patch(`http://localhost:3001/users/${user.id}`, {
      notifs: updatedNotifs,
    })
  }

  if (actionType.value === 'publish') {
    if (!selectedVolume.value) {
      alert('Р’С‹Р±РµСЂРёС‚Рµ С‚РѕРј')
      return
    }

    /* Р•СЃР»Рё РѕРїСѓР±Р»РёРєРѕРІС‹РІР°РµС‚СЃСЏ СЂСѓРєРѕРїРёСЃСЊ*/
    const volume = volumes.value.find((v) => v.volId === selectedVolume.value)

    const updatedManuscripts = [...(volume.manuscripts || []), props.manuscript.id]

    await axios.patch(`http://localhost:3001/volumes/${volume.id}`, {
      manuscripts: updatedManuscripts,
    })
  }
  showActionModal.value = false
  location.reload()
}

/* Р•СЃР»Рё СЂСѓРєРѕРїРёСЃСЊ РѕРїСѓР±Р»РёРєРѕРІР°РЅР° РёР»Рё РѕС‚РєР»РѕРЅРµРЅР°, С‚Рѕ РµС‘ СѓР¶Рµ РЅРµР»СЊР·СЏ РёР·РјРµРЅРёС‚СЊ */
const isReadonly = computed(() => {
  return props.manuscript.stage === 'РћРїСѓР±Р»РёРєРѕРІР°РЅРѕ' || props.manuscript.stage === 'РћС‚РєР»РѕРЅРµРЅРѕ'
})

/* РЈРґР°Р»РµРЅРёРµ Р·Р°РґР°С‡Рё, СЃРІСЏР·Р°РЅРЅРѕР№ СЃ СЂСѓРєРѕРїРёСЃСЊСЋ */
const deleteTaskByManuscript = async () => {
  const tasksRes = await axios.get('http://localhost:3001/tasks')

  const task = tasksRes.data.find((t) => Number(t.manuscriptId) === Number(props.manuscript.id))

  if (task) {
    await axios.delete(`http://localhost:3001/tasks/${task.id}`)
  }
}
</script>

<template>
  <div>
    <!-- РћРїРёСЃР°РЅРёРµ -->
    <div class="text-blocks">
      <div class="section-name big">РћРїРёСЃР°РЅРёРµ</div>
      <div class="section-text">
        {{ manuscript.description }}
      </div>

      <!-- РљР»СЋС‡РµРІС‹Рµ СЃР»РѕРІР° -->
      <div class="section-text">
        РљР»СЋС‡РµРІС‹Рµ СЃР»РѕРІР°:
        <ul>
          <li v-for="word in manuscript.keyWords" :key="word">
            {{ word }}
          </li>
        </ul>
      </div>

      <!-- РњР°С‚РµСЂРёР°Р»С‹ -->
      <div class="section-name big">РњР°С‚РµСЂРёР°Р»С‹</div>
      <div class="section-text">
        Р¤Р°Р№Р»С‹ СЂСѓРєРѕРїРёСЃРё:
        <div>
          <ul v-if="manuscript.manuscriptFiles?.length">
            <li v-for="(file, index) in manuscript.manuscriptFiles" :key="file">
              <a
                :href="file.replace('./public', '')"
                target="_blank"
                class="materials-block editor"
              >
                {{ file.split('/').pop() }}
              </a>
              <template v-if="!isReadonly">
                <ReplaceFileButton :index="index" :manuscript="manuscript" />
                <DeleteFileButton :index="index" @ask-delete="openDeleteOverlay" />
              </template>
            </li>
          </ul>
          <AddManuscriptFileButton v-if="!isReadonly" :manuscript="manuscript" />
        </div>
      </div>

      <!-- РЎРѕРїСЂРѕРІРѕРґРёС‚РµР»СЊРЅРѕРµ РїРёСЃСЊРјРѕ -->
      <div class="section-text">
        РЎРѕРїСЂРѕРІРѕРґРёС‚РµР»СЊРЅРѕРµ РїРёСЃСЊРјРѕ:
        <div>
          <ul v-if="manuscript.coverLetter?.length">
            <li v-for="file in manuscript.coverLetter" :key="file">
              <a
                :href="file.replace('./public', '')"
                target="_blank"
                class="materials-block editor"
              >
                {{ file.split('/').pop() }}
              </a>
            </li>
          </ul>

<script setup>
import { ref } from 'vue'
import axios from 'axios'
import VueFeather from 'vue-feather'

const props = defineProps({
  index: Number,
  manuscript: Object,
})

const fileInput = ref(null)

const openFileDialog = () => {
  fileInput.value.click()
}

const handleFileChange = async (event) => {
  const file = event.target.files[0]
  if (!file) return

  const newPath = `./public/manuscripts/${file.name}`

  const files = [...props.manuscript.manuscriptFiles]
  files[props.index] = newPath

  await axios.patch(`http://localhost:3001/manuscripts/${props.manuscript.id}`, {
    manuscriptFiles: files,
    version: props.manuscript.version + 1,
  })

  location.reload()
}
</script>

<template>
  <button class="general-button editor" @click="openFileDialog">
    РР·РјРµРЅРёС‚СЊ С„Р°Р№Р»
    <vue-feather type="edit-3" size="20" />
  </button>
  <input ref="fileInput" type="file" hidden @change="handleFileChange" />
</template>

<script setup>
import { ref, computed } from 'vue'
import axios from 'axios'
import VueFeather from 'vue-feather'

import ChapterDropdown from '@/components/editor/manuscripts/ChapterDropdown.vue'

const props = defineProps({
  authors: Array,
  redactors: Array,
})

const emit = defineEmits(['saved'])

const title = ref('')
const description = ref('')
const keywords = ref('')
const chapter = ref('')

const authorSearch = ref('')
const selectedAuthors = ref([])

const manuscriptFiles = ref([])
const coverLetterFiles = ref([])

const validationError = ref(false)

/*РџСЂРѕРІРµСЂРєР° С„РѕСЂРјС‹ РЅР° Р·Р°РїРѕР»РµРЅРёРµ РѕР±СЏР·Р°С‚РµР»СЊРЅС‹С… РїРѕР»РµР№*/
function isFormValid() {
  return (
    title.value.trim() &&
    description.value.trim() &&
    keywords.value.trim() &&
    chapter.value.trim() &&
    selectedAuthors.value.length > 0
  )
}

/*Р—Р°РіСЂСѓР·РєР° С„Р°Р№Р»РѕРІ СЂСѓРєРѕРїРёСЃРё*/
function handleManuscriptFiles(e) {
  const newFiles = Array.from(e.target.files)

  const existingNames = manuscriptFiles.value.map((f) => f.name)

  const uniqueFiles = newFiles.filter((f) => !existingNames.includes(f.name))

  manuscriptFiles.value = [...manuscriptFiles.value, ...uniqueFiles]

  e.target.value = ''
}

/*Р—Р°РіСЂСѓР·РєР° С„Р°Р№Р»РѕРІ СЃРѕРїСЂРѕРІРѕРґРёС‚РµР»СЊРЅРѕРіРѕ РїРёСЃСЊРјР°*/
function handleCoverLetterFiles(e) {
  const newFiles = Array.from(e.target.files)

  const existingNames = coverLetterFiles.value.map((f) => f.name)

  const uniqueFiles = newFiles.filter((f) => !existingNames.includes(f.name))

  coverLetterFiles.value = [...coverLetterFiles.value, ...uniqueFiles]

  e.target.value = ''
}

/* РџРѕРёСЃРє Р°РІС‚РѕСЂР° РїРѕ РёРјРµРЅРё РёР»Рё РїРѕС‡С‚Рµ*/
const filteredAuthors = computed(() => {
  if (!authorSearch.value) return props.authors

  const query = authorSearch.value.toLowerCase()

  return props.authors.filter(
    (a) => a.name.toLowerCase().includes(query) || a.mail.toLowerCase().includes(query),
  )
})

/* РЈРґР°Р»РµРЅРёРµ РґРѕР±Р°РІР»РµРЅРЅРѕРіРѕ С„Р°Р№Р»Р° */
function removeManuscriptFile(fileName) {
  manuscriptFiles.value = manuscriptFiles.value.filter((f) => f.name !== fileName)
}

/* РЈРґР°Р»РµРЅРёРµ СЃРѕРїСЂРѕРІРѕРґРёС‚РµР»СЊРЅРѕРіРѕ РїРёСЃСЊРјР° */
function removeCoverLetterFile(fileName) {
  coverLetterFiles.value = coverLetterFiles.value.filter((f) => f.name !== fileName)
}

/*РЎРѕС…СЂР°РЅРµРЅРёРµ СЂСѓРєРѕРїРёСЃРё*/
async function saveManuscript() {
  /*РџСЂРѕРІРµСЂРєР° РЅР° Р·Р°РїРѕР»РЅРµРЅРёРµ РѕР±СЏР·Р°С‚РµР»СЊРЅС‹С… РїРѕР»РµР№*/
  if (!isFormValid()) {
    validationError.value = true
    return
  }

  /*Р”РѕР±Р°РІР»РµРЅРёРµ РЅР°С‡Р°Р»СЊРЅС‹С… СЃРѕР±С‹С‚РёР№*/
  validationError.value = false

  const manuscriptId = Date.now()

  const today = new Date().toISOString().slice(0, 10)

  const events = [
    {
      event: 'РЎС‚Р°С‚СЊСЏ РїРѕР»СѓС‡РµРЅР°',
      user: 0,
      date: today,
      version: 1,
    },
    {
      event: 'Р“Р»Р°РІРЅС‹Р№ СЂРµРґР°РєС‚РѕСЂ Р•РІРіРµРЅРёР№ Р¤С‘РґРѕСЂРѕРІ РЅР°Р·РЅР°С‡РµРЅ',
      user: 0,
      date: today,
      version: 1,
    },
  ]

  /* РћР±РЅРѕРІР»РµРЅРёРµ manuscripts РІ db.json*/
  const manuscript = {
    manuscriptId,
    stage: 'Р’ РїСЂРѕС†РµСЃСЃРµ',
    redactorStatus: 'mine',
    redactorsId: [1],
    title: title.value,
    authorsId: selectedAuthors.value,
    keyWords: keywords.value.split(','),
    description: description.value,
    chapter: chapter.value,
    manuscriptFiles: manuscriptFiles.value.map((f) => `/public/manuscripts/${f.name}`),
    coverLetter: coverLetterFiles.value.map((f) => `/public/manuscripts/${f.name}`),
    sentDate: new Date().toISOString().slice(0, 10),
    version: 1,
    events: events,
  }

  const res = await axios.post('http://localhost:3001/manuscripts', manuscript)

  const newManuscript = res.data

  /* РћР±РЅРѕРІР»РµРЅРёРµ tasks РІ db.json*/
  const task = {
    taskId: `Р›Рљ-${Date.now()}`,
    manuscriptId: newManuscript.manuscriptId,
    createdAt: newManuscript.sentDate,
    type: 'РќР°Р№С‚Рё Рё РїСЂРёРіР»Р°СЃРёС‚СЊ СЂРµС†РµРЅР·РµРЅС‚РѕРІ',
    stage: 'Р РµС†РµРЅР·РµРЅС‚С‹ РЅРµ РїСЂРёРіР»Р°С€РµРЅС‹',
    invitedReviewersId: [],
    acceptedReviewersId: [],
    declinedReviewersId: [],
    reviewesId: [],
  }

  await axios.post('http://localhost:3001/tasks', task)

  emit('saved')
}
</script>

<template>
  <div class="modals form">
    <div class="required-note" :class="{ error: validationError }">* вЂ” РѕР±СЏР·Р°С‚РµР»СЊРЅС‹Рµ РїРѕР»СЏ</div>

    <!-- РџРѕР»Рµ РЅР°Р·РІР°РЅРёСЏ -->
    <input
      v-model="title"
      placeholder="РќР°Р·РІР°РЅРёРµ СЃС‚Р°С‚СЊРё*"
      class="input"
      :class="{ invalid: validationError && !title }"
    />

    <!-- РџРѕР»Рµ РѕРїРёСЃР°РЅРёСЏ -->
    <textarea
      v-model="description"
      placeholder="РћРїРёСЃР°РЅРёРµ*"
      class="input"
      :class="{ invalid: validationError && !title }"
    />

    <!-- РџРѕР»Рµ РєР»СЋС‡РµРІС‹С… СЃР»РѕРІ -->
    <input
      v-model="keywords"
      placeholder="РљР»СЋС‡РµРІС‹Рµ СЃР»РѕРІР° (С‡РµСЂРµР· Р·Р°РїСЏС‚СѓСЋ)*"
      class="input"
      :class="{ invalid: validationError && !title }"
    />

    <!-- РџРѕР»Рµ СЂР°Р·РґРµР»Р° -->
    <div class="section-name">Р Р°Р·РґРµР»*</div>

    <ChapterDropdown v-model="chapter" />

    <div v-if="validationError && !chapter" class="error-text">Р’С‹Р±РµСЂРёС‚Рµ СЂР°Р·РґРµР»</div>

    <!-- РџРѕРёСЃРє Р°РІС‚РѕСЂРѕРІ -->
    <div class="section-name">РђРІС‚РѕСЂС‹*</div>
    <input
      v-model="authorSearch"
      placeholder="РџРѕРёСЃРє Р°РІС‚РѕСЂР° РїРѕ РёРјРµРЅРё РёР»Рё РїРѕС‡С‚Рµ"
      class="search-input-small"
    />
    <div class="modal-containers">
      <label v-for="author in filteredAuthors" :key="author.id">
        <div class="modal-cards">
          <input type="checkbox" :value="author.authorsId" v-model="selectedAuthors" />
          {{ author.name }} | {{ author.mail }}
        </div>
      </label>
    </div>

    <!-- Р—Р°РіСЂСѓР·РєР° С„Р°Р№Р»РѕРІ -->
    <div>
      <div class="section-name" style="margin-bottom: 8px">Р¤Р°Р№Р»С‹ СЂСѓРєРѕРїРёСЃРё*</div>
      <label class="general-button editor">
        Р’С‹Р±СЂР°С‚СЊ С„Р°Р№Р»С‹<input type="file" multiple @change="handleManuscriptFiles" hidden />
      </label>
      <div v-if="manuscriptFiles.length" class="materials-block">
        <div v-for="file in manuscriptFiles" :key="file.name">
          <span>{{ file.name }}</span
          ><button @click="removeManuscriptFile(file.name)" style="margin-left: 8px">вњ•</button>
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>

    <div>
      <div class="section-name" style="margin-bottom: 8px">РЎРѕРїСЂРѕРІРѕРґРёС‚РµР»СЊРЅРѕРµ РїРёСЃСЊРјРѕ</div>
      <label class="general-button editor">

<script setup>
import { ref } from 'vue'
import VueFeather from 'vue-feather'

defineProps({
  modelValue: String,
})

const emit = defineEmits(['update:modelValue'])

const isOpen = ref(false)

const chapters = [
  'РњР°С‚РµРјР°С‚РёРєР°',
  'РњРµС…Р°РЅРёРєР°',
  'Р¤РёР·РёРєР°',
  'РђСЃС‚СЂРѕРЅРѕРјРёСЏ',
  'РРЅС„РѕСЂРјР°С‚РёРєР°, РІС‹С‡РёСЃР»РёС‚РµР»СЊРЅР°СЏ С‚РµС…РЅРёРєР° Рё СѓРїСЂР°РІР»РµРЅРёРµ',
  'РњР°С‚РµРјР°С‚РёС‡РµСЃРєРѕРµ РјРѕРґРµР»РёСЂРѕРІР°РЅРёРµ',
  'Р”РёРЅР°РјРёРєР° РјРЅРѕРіРѕС„Р°Р·РЅС‹С… СЃСЂРµРґ',
  'РџРѕР·РґСЂР°РІРёС‚РµР»СЊРЅС‹Рµ СЃС‚Р°С‚СЊРё',
]

function toggle() {
  isOpen.value = !isOpen.value
}

function select(chapter) {
  emit('update:modelValue', chapter)
  isOpen.value = false
}
</script>

<template>
  <div class="sort-wrapper">
    <!-- РљРЅРѕРїРєР° -->
    <button class="sort-btn" @click="toggle">
      {{ modelValue || 'Р’С‹Р±РµСЂРёС‚Рµ СЂР°Р·РґРµР»*' }}

      <vue-feather
        type="chevron-down"
        size="20"
        :style="{ transform: isOpen ? 'rotate(180deg)' : 'rotate(0)' }"
      />
    </button>

    <!-- Р’С‹РїР°РґР°СЋС‰РёР№ СЃРїРёСЃРѕРє -->
    <div v-if="isOpen" class="dropdown">
      <label v-for="chapter in chapters" :key="chapter" class="option">
        <input
          type="radio"
          :value="chapter"

          @change="select(chapter)"

=== FILE: \src\components\editor\manuscripts\EditorFilter.vue ===
<script setup>
import { ref, computed } from 'vue'
import VueFeather from 'vue-feather'

const props = defineProps({
  editors: Array,
  selectedEditors: Array,
  mode: String,
})

const emit = defineEmits(['change-editor-filter'])

const isOpen = ref(false)
const search = ref('')

const toggle = () => {
  isOpen.value = !isOpen.value
}

const selectMode = (value) => {
  emit('change-editor-filter', {
    mode: value,
    editors: [],
  })

  isOpen.value = false
}

const filteredEditors = computed(() => {
  if (!search.value) return props.editors

  return props.editors.filter((e) => e.name.toLowerCase().includes(search.value.toLowerCase()))
})

const toggleEditor = (id) => {
  const current = [...props.selectedEditors]

  if (current.includes(id)) {
    emit('change-editor-filter', {
      mode: 'by_name',
      editors: current.filter((e) => e !== id),
    })
  } else {
    current.push(id)

    emit('change-editor-filter', {
      mode: 'by_name',
      editors: current,
    })
  }
}
</script>

<template>
  <div class="sort-wrapper">
    <button class="sort-btn" @click="toggle">
      Р¤РёР»СЊС‚СЂ СЂРµРґР°РєС‚РѕСЂР°

      <vue-feather
        type="chevron-down"
        size="20"
        :style="{ transform: isOpen ? 'rotate(180deg)' : 'rotate(0)' }"
      />
    </button>

    <div v-if="isOpen" class="dropdown">
      <label class="option">
        <input type="radio" value="all" :checked="mode === 'all'" @change="selectMode('all')" />
        Р’СЃРµ
      </label>

      <label class="option">
        <input type="radio" value="mine" :checked="mode === 'mine'" @change="selectMode('mine')" />
        Р’С‹
      </label>

      <label class="option">
        <input type="radio" value="none" :checked="mode === 'none'" @change="selectMode('none')" />
        Р‘РµР· СЂРµРґР°РєС‚РѕСЂР°
      </label>

      <label class="option">
        <input
          type="radio"
          value="by_name"
          :checked="mode === 'by_name'"
          @change="selectMode('by_name')"
        />
        РџРѕ С„Р°РјРёР»РёРё
      </label>

      <div v-if="mode === 'by_name'" class="editor-search">
        <input v-model="search" class="search-input-small" placeholder="РџРѕРёСЃРє СЂРµРґР°РєС‚РѕСЂР°" />

        <div class="editor-list">
          <label v-for="editor in filteredEditors" :key="editor.redactorsId" class="option">
            <input
              type="checkbox"
              :checked="selectedEditors.includes(editor.redactorsId)"
              @change="toggleEditor(editor.redactorsId)"
            />

            {{ editor.name }}
          </label>
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  </div>
</template>

=== FILE: \src\components\editor\taskDetails\TaskDetailsContainer.vue ===
<script setup>
import { computed } from 'vue'
import axios from 'axios'
import { ref } from 'vue'

import ChapterSortDropdown from './ChapterSortDropdown.vue'
import CountSortDropdown from './CountSortDropdown.vue'

const showConfirm = ref(false)
const confirmType = ref(null) // cancel | repeat
const selectedReviewer = ref(null)

const showReviewerSearch = ref(false)

const searchQuery = ref('')
const countFilter = ref('all')
const chapterFilter = ref('all')

const props = defineProps({
  activeTab: String,
  task: Object,
  manuscript: Object,
  authors: Array,
  reviewers: Array,
  reviews: Array,
  users: Array,
})

/* Р РµС†РµРЅР·РёРё Рє СЂСѓРєРѕРїРёСЃРё */
const manuscriptReviews = computed(() => {
  return props.reviews.filter((r) => Number(r.manuscriptId) === Number(props.manuscript.id))
})

/* Р РµС†РµРЅР·РёРё СЃ РёРјРµРЅР°РјРё СЂРµС†РµРЅР·РµРЅС‚РѕРІ */
const reviewsWithReviewer = computed(() => {
  return manuscriptReviews.value.map((review) => {
    const reviewer = props.reviewers.find(
      (rev) => Number(rev.reviewersId) === Number(review.reviewersId),
    )

    return {
      ...review,
      reviewerName: reviewer ? reviewer.name : 'РќРµРёР·РІРµСЃС‚РЅС‹Р№ СЂРµС†РµРЅР·РµРЅС‚',
      reviewerMail: reviewer ? reviewer.mail : '',
    }
  })
})

/* РџРѕР»СѓС‡Р°РµРј Р°РІС‚РѕСЂРѕРІ */
const manuscriptAuthors = computed(() => {
  return props.manuscript.authorsId.map((id) => props.authors.find((a) => a.authorsId === id))
})

/* РџРѕР»СѓС‡Р°РµРј СЂРµС†РµРЅР·РµРЅС‚РѕРІ */
const getReviewersByIds = (ids) => {
  return props.reviewers.filter((reviewer) => ids.includes(reviewer.reviewersId))
}

/* РџСЂРёРЅСЏРІС€РёРµ СЂРµС†РµРЅР·РµРЅС‚С‹*/
const acceptedReviewers = computed(() => getReviewersByIds(props.task.acceptedReviewersId || []))

/* РћС‚РєР»РѕРЅРёРІС€РёРµ СЂРµС†РµРЅР·РµРЅС‚С‹*/
const declinedReviewers = computed(() => getReviewersByIds(props.task.declinedReviewersId || []))

/* РџСЂРёРіР»Р°С€С‘РЅРЅС‹Рµ (РµС‰С‘ РЅРµ РѕС‚РІРµС‚РёР»Рё) */
const invitedPendingReviewers = computed(() => {
  const invited = props.task.invitedReviewersId || []
  const accepted = props.task.acceptedReviewersId || []
  const declined = props.task.declinedReviewersId || []

  const pendingIds = invited.filter((id) => !accepted.includes(id) && !declined.includes(id))

  return getReviewersByIds(pendingIds)
})

/* РћС‚РєСЂС‹С‚РёРµ РѕРІРµСЂР»РµСЏ СѓРІРµСЂРµРЅРЅРѕСЃС‚Рё */
const openCancelConfirm = (reviewer) => {
  confirmType.value = 'cancel'
  selectedReviewer.value = reviewer
  showConfirm.value = true
}

const openRepeatConfirm = (reviewer) => {
  confirmType.value = 'repeat'
  selectedReviewer.value = reviewer
  showConfirm.value = true
}

/* Р—Р°РєСЂС‹С‚РёРµ РѕРІРµСЂР»РµСЏ СѓРІРµСЂРµРЅРЅРѕСЃС‚Рё */
const closeConfirm = () => {
  showConfirm.value = false
  selectedReviewer.value = null
}

/* Р§С‚РѕР±С‹ РјРµРЅСЏР»РѕСЃСЊ db.json */
const confirmAction = async () => {
  if (!selectedReviewer.value) return

  const reviewerId = selectedReviewer.value.reviewersId

  const task = { ...props.task }

  if (confirmType.value === 'cancel') {
    task.invitedReviewersId = task.invitedReviewersId.filter((id) => id !== reviewerId)

    task.acceptedReviewersId = task.acceptedReviewersId.filter((id) => id !== reviewerId)
  }

  if (confirmType.value === 'repeat') {
    task.declinedReviewersId = task.declinedReviewersId.filter((id) => id !== reviewerId)

    if (!task.invitedReviewersId.includes(reviewerId)) {
      task.invitedReviewersId.push(reviewerId)
    }
  }

  await axios.patch(`http://localhost:3001/tasks/${task.id}`, task)

  closeConfirm()

  location.reload()
}

/* РћРІРµСЂР»РµР№ РїРѕРёСЃРєР° СЂРµС†РµРЅР·РµРЅС‚РѕРІ */
const filteredReviewers = computed(() => {
  let result = props.reviewers

  // РїРѕРёСЃРє
  if (searchQuery.value) {
    const query = searchQuery.value.toLowerCase()

    result = result.filter((r) => {
      return r.name.toLowerCase().includes(query) || r.mail.toLowerCase().includes(query)
    })
  }

  // С„РёР»СЊС‚СЂ РїРѕ РєРѕР»РёС‡РµСЃС‚РІСѓ СЂР°Р±РѕС‚
  if (countFilter.value !== 'all') {
    result = result.filter((r) => {
      const count = r.countReviews

      if (countFilter.value === 'lt10') return count < 10
      if (countFilter.value === '10-50') return count >= 10 && count <= 50
      if (countFilter.value === '50-100') return count > 50 && count <= 100
      if (countFilter.value === 'gt100') return count > 100
    })
  }

  // С„РёР»СЊС‚СЂ РїРѕ РЅР°РїСЂР°РІР»РµРЅРёСЋ
  if (chapterFilter.value !== 'all') {
    result = result.filter((r) => r.mainChapter === chapterFilter.value)
  }

  return result
})

const setCountFilter = (value) => {
  countFilter.value = value
}

const setChapterFilter = (value) => {
  chapterFilter.value = value
}

/* РџСЂРёРіР»Р°С€Р°РµРј СЂРµС†РµРЅР·РµРЅС‚Р° */
const inviteReviewer = async (reviewer) => {
  const reviewerId = reviewer.reviewersId

  const task = { ...props.task }

  if (task.invitedReviewersId.includes(reviewerId)) {
    alert('Р­С‚РѕС‚ СЂРµС†РµРЅР·РµРЅС‚ СѓР¶Рµ РїСЂРёРіР»Р°С€С‘РЅ')
    return
  }

  // РґРѕР±Р°РІР»СЏРµРј СЂРµС†РµРЅР·РµРЅС‚Р° РІ Р·Р°РґР°С‡Сѓ
  task.invitedReviewersId.push(reviewerId)

  await axios.patch(`http://localhost:3001/tasks/${task.id}`, task)

  // Р”РѕР±Р°РІР»СЏРµРј СЃРѕР±С‹С‚РёРµ РІ СЂСѓРєРѕРїРёСЃСЊ
  const manuscript = { ...props.manuscript }

  // Р•СЃР»Рё РІРґСЂСѓРі events РЅРµС‚
  if (!manuscript.events) {
    manuscript.events = []
  }

  // Р”РѕР±Р°РІР»РµРЅРёРµ СЃРѕР±С‹С‚РёСЏ
  manuscript.events.push({
    event: 'Р РµС†РµРЅР·РµРЅС‚С‹ РїСЂРёРіР»Р°С€РµРЅС‹',
    user: 1,
    date: new Date().toISOString().split('T')[0],
    version: manuscript.version,
  })

  await axios.patch(`http://localhost:3001/manuscripts/${manuscript.id}`, manuscript)

  showReviewerSearch.value = false
  location.reload()
}

/* РџРѕР»СѓС‡РёС‚СЊ РёРјСЏ РїРѕР»СЊР·РѕРІР°С‚РµР»СЏ РїРѕ id */
const getUserName = (id) => {
  const user = props.users.find((u) => Number(u.userId) === Number(id))
  return user ? user.name : 'РќРµРёР·РІРµСЃС‚РЅС‹Р№ РїРѕР»СЊР·РѕРІР°С‚РµР»СЊ'
}

/* Р¤РѕСЂРјРёСЂСѓРµРј С‚Р°Р±Р»РёС†Сѓ РёСЃС‚РѕСЂРёРё */
const groupedEvents = computed(() => {
  if (!props.manuscript?.events) return []

  const groups = {}

  props.manuscript.events.forEach((event) => {
    const version = event.version || 1

    if (!groups[version]) {
      groups[version] = []
    }

    groups[version].push(event)
  })

  // СЃРѕСЂС‚РёСЂРѕРІРєР° СЃРѕР±С‹С‚РёР№ РІРЅСѓС‚СЂРё РІРµСЂСЃРёРё
  Object.keys(groups).forEach((version) => {
    groups[version].sort((a, b) => new Date(a.date) - new Date(b.date))
  })

  // СЃРѕСЂС‚РёСЂСѓРµРј СЃРѕР±С‹С‚РёСЏ РїРѕ РІРµСЂСЃРёРё
  return Object.keys(groups)
    .sort((a, b) => Number(a) - Number(b))
    .map((version) => ({
      version,
      events: groups[version],
    }))
})
</script>

<script setup>
import { ref } from 'vue'
import VueFeather from 'vue-feather'

const { sortOrder } = defineProps({
  sortOrder: String,
})

const emit = defineEmits(['change-sort'])

const isOpen = ref(false)

const toggle = () => {
  isOpen.value = !isOpen.value
}

const selectOption = (value) => {
  emit('change-sort', value)
  isOpen.value = false
}
</script>

<template>
  <div class="sort-wrapper">
    <button class="sort-btn" @click="toggle">
      {{ sortOrder === 'new' ? 'РЎРЅР°С‡Р°Р»Р° РЅРѕРІС‹Рµ' : 'РЎРЅР°С‡Р°Р»Р° СЃС‚Р°СЂС‹Рµ' }}

      <vue-feather
        type="chevron-down"
        size="20"
        :style="{ transform: isOpen ? 'rotate(180deg)' : 'rotate(0)' }"
      />
    </button>

    <div v-if="isOpen" class="dropdown">
      <label class="option">
        <input
          type="radio"
          value="new"
          :checked="sortOrder === 'new'"
          @change="selectOption('new')"
        />
        РЎРЅР°С‡Р°Р»Р° РЅРѕРІС‹Рµ
      </label>

      <label class="option">
        <input
          type="radio"
          value="old"
          :checked="sortOrder === 'old'"
          @change="selectOption('old')"
        />
        РЎРЅР°С‡Р°Р»Р° СЃС‚Р°СЂС‹Рµ
      </label>
    </div>
  </div>
</template>

const { activeFilter, sortOrder } = defineProps({
  activeFilter: String,
  sortOrder: String,
})

const emit = defineEmits(['change-filter', 'change-sort'])

  emit('change-filter', filter)
}

const setSort = (order) => {
  emit('change-sort', order)
}

  <div class="actions-bar">
    <div class="filters">
      <button class="filter" :class="{ active: activeFilter === 'all' }" @click="setFilter('all')">

      </button>

      <button
        class="filter"
        :class="{ active: activeFilter === 'РћС†РµРЅРёС‚СЊ РѕС‚Р·С‹РІС‹ СЂРµС†РµРЅР·РµРЅС‚РѕРІ' }"
        @click="setFilter('РћС†РµРЅРёС‚СЊ РѕС‚Р·С‹РІС‹ СЂРµС†РµРЅР·РµРЅС‚РѕРІ')"
      >
        РћС†РµРЅРёС‚СЊ РѕС‚Р·С‹РІС‹ СЂРµС†РµРЅР·РµРЅС‚РѕРІ
      </button>

      <button
        class="filter"
        :class="{ active: activeFilter === 'РќР°Р№С‚Рё Рё РїСЂРёРіР»Р°СЃРёС‚СЊ СЂРµС†РµРЅР·РµРЅС‚РѕРІ' }"
        @click="setFilter('РќР°Р№С‚Рё Рё РїСЂРёРіР»Р°СЃРёС‚СЊ СЂРµС†РµРЅР·РµРЅС‚РѕРІ')"
      >
        РќР°Р№С‚Рё Рё РїСЂРёРіР»Р°СЃРёС‚СЊ СЂРµС†РµРЅР·РµРЅС‚РѕРІ
      </button>
    </div>

    <SortDropdown :sortOrder="sortOrder" @change-sort="setSort" />

<style scoped>
.actions-bar {

=== FILE: \src\components\editor\tasks\TasksContainer.vue ===
<script setup>
import { computed } from 'vue'
import TaskCard from './TaskCard.vue'

const props = defineProps({
  tasks: Array,
  manuscripts: Array,
  authors: Array,
  reviewers: Array,
  reviews: Array,
})

/* Р¤РѕСЂРјРёСЂСѓРµРј Р·Р°РґР°С‡Рё*/
const preparedTasks = computed(() => {
  return props.tasks.map((task) => {
    const manuscript = props.manuscripts.find((m) => m.manuscriptId === task.manuscriptId)

    const authorsList = manuscript
      ? props.authors.filter((a) => manuscript.authorsId.includes(a.authorsId))
      : []

    return {
      id: task.id,
      taskId: task.taskId,
      stage: task.stage,
      type: task.type,
      createdAt: task.createdAt,
      version: manuscript?.version,

      manuscriptTitle: manuscript?.title,
      authors: authorsList,
      manuscriptSentDate: manuscript?.sentDate,

      invitedCount: task.invitedReviewersId.length,
      acceptedCount: task.acceptedReviewersId.length,
      declinedCount: task.declinedReviewersId.length,
      reportsCount: task.reviewesId.length,
    }
  })
})
</script>

<template>
  <div class="containers">
    <TaskCard v-for="task in preparedTasks" :key="task.id" :task="task" />
  </div>
</template>

import { createRouter, createWebHistory } from 'vue-router'
import EditorLayout from '../layouts/EditorLayout.vue'
import TaskView from '../views/TasksView.vue'
import ManuscriptsView from '../views/ManuscriptsView.vue'
import MainLayout from '../layouts/MainLayout.vue'
import MainView from '../views/MainView.vue'

import MainSectionsLayout from '@/layouts/MainSectionsLayout.vue'
import MainManuscriptView from '@/views/MainManuscriptView.vue'

import AboutGournalPage from '@/views/AboutGournalPage.vue'
import AboutGournalDetailsView from '@/views/AboutGournalDetailsView.vue'

const router = createRouter({
  history: createWebHistory(import.meta.env.BASE_URL),
  routes: [
    {
      path: '/',
      component: MainLayout,
      children: [
        {
          path: '',
          component: MainView,
        },
      ],
    },
    {
      path: '/manuscripts',
      component: MainSectionsLayout,
      children: [
        {
          path: 'view/:id',
          component: MainManuscriptView,
        },
      ],
    },
    {
      path: '/about',
      component: MainSectionsLayout,
      children: [
        {
          path: '',
          component: AboutGournalPage,
        },
        {
          path: ':section',
          component: AboutGournalDetailsView,
        },
      ],
    },
    {
      path: '/editor',
      component: EditorLayout,
      children: [
        {
          path: 'tasks',
          component: TaskView,
        },
        {
          path: 'tasks/:id',
          component: () => import('../views/TaskDetailsView.vue'),
        },
        {
          path: 'manuscripts',
          component: ManuscriptsView,
        },
        {
          path: 'manuscripts/:id',
          component: () => import('../views/ManuscriptsDetailView.vue'),
        },
        {
          path: 'analytics',
          component: () => import('../views/AnalyticsView.vue'),
        },
      ],
    },
  ],
})

export default router

=== FILE: \src\stores\counter.js ===
import { ref, computed } from 'vue'
import { defineStore } from 'pinia'

export const useCounterStore = defineStore('counter', () => {
  const count = ref(0)
  const doubleCount = computed(() => count.value * 2)
  function increment() {
    count.value++
  }

  return { count, doubleCount, increment }
})

=== FILE: \src\views\AboutGournalDetailsView.vue ===
<script setup>
import { computed, ref, onMounted } from 'vue'
import { useRoute } from 'vue-router'
import axios from 'axios'

import EditorialSection from '@/components/main_page/main_sections/aboutGournal/EditorialSection.vue'
import PeopleSection from '@/components/main_page/main_sections/aboutGournal/PeopleSection.vue'

import SectionsPolicySection from '@/components/main_page/main_sections/aboutGournal/SectionsPolicySection.vue'

import SendOnlineSection from '@/components/main_page/main_sections/aboutGournal/SendOnlineSection.vue'

import SiteMapSection from '@/components/main_page/main_sections/aboutGournal/SiteMapSection.vue'
import SystemSection from '@/components/main_page/main_sections/aboutGournal/SystemSection.vue'
import SponsorsSection from '@/components/main_page/main_sections/aboutGournal/SponsorsSection.vue'

const route = useRoute()

const section = computed(() => route.params.section)

const editorialAdvice = ref([])
const editorialCollegium = ref([])

onMounted(async () => {
  const [redactorsRes, adviceRes, collegiumRes] = await Promise.all([
    axios.get('http://localhost:3001/redactors'),
    axios.get('http://localhost:3001/editoralAdvice'),
    axios.get('http://localhost:3001/editoralCollegium'),
  ])

  const redactors = redactorsRes.data

  const adviceIds = adviceRes.data[0]?.redactorsId || []
  const collegiumIds = collegiumRes.data[0]?.redactorsId || []

  editorialAdvice.value = redactors.filter((r) => adviceIds.includes(r.redactorsId))

  editorialCollegium.value = redactors.filter((r) => collegiumIds.includes(r.redactorsId))
})
</script>

<template>
  <div class="page">
    <EditorialSection
      v-if="section === 'editorial'"
      :editorialAdvice="editorialAdvice"
      :editorialCollegium="editorialCollegium"
    />
    <PeopleSection v-else-if="section === 'people'" />

    <SectionsPolicySection v-else-if="section === 'sections-policy'" />
    <SectionsPolicySection v-else-if="section === 'plagiarism'" />
    <SectionsPolicySection v-else-if="section === 'open-access'" />

    <SendOnlineSection v-else-if="section === 'submission'" />
    <SendOnlineSection v-else-if="section === 'authors-guide'" />
    <SendOnlineSection v-else-if="section === 'review-rules'" />
    <SendOnlineSection v-else-if="section === 'privacy'" />

    <SiteMapSection v-else-if="section === 'site-map'" />
    <SystemSection v-else-if="section === 'system'" />
    <SponsorsSection v-else-if="section === 'sponsors'" />
  </div>
</template>

<script setup>
import { ref, onMounted } from 'vue'
import { useRoute } from 'vue-router'
import axios from 'axios'

import ManuscriptPdfViewer from '@/components/main_page/main_sections/manuscripts/ManuscriptPdfViewer.vue'
import ManuscriptSidebar from '@/components/main_page/main_sections/manuscripts/ManuscriptSidebar.vue'
import ManuscriptActions from '@/components/main_page/main_sections/manuscripts/ManuscriptActions.vue'

const route = useRoute()

const manuscript = ref(null)
const authors = ref([])
const activeFile = ref(null)

onMounted(async () => {
  const [manuscriptsRes, authorsRes] = await Promise.all([
    axios.get('http://localhost:3001/manuscripts'),
    axios.get('http://localhost:3001/authors'),
  ])

  const id = Number(route.params.id)

  manuscript.value = manuscriptsRes.data.find((m) => m.manuscriptId === id)

  if (manuscript.value) {
    authors.value = authorsRes.data.filter((a) => manuscript.value.authorsId.includes(a.authorsId))

    activeFile.value = manuscript.value.manuscriptFiles?.[0]
  }
})
</script>

<template>
  <div div v-if="manuscript" class="contents main-sections header-section">
    <div class="text-blocks">
      <div class="title Lora-family large" style="color: white">РџСЂРѕСЃРјРѕС‚СЂ СЂСѓРєРѕРїРёСЃРё</div>
    </div>
  </div>
  <div v-if="manuscript" class="contents main-sections content">
    <div class="text-blocks">
      <div class="title Lora-family large">
        {{ manuscript.title }}
      </div>
    </div>

    <ManuscriptActions />

    <div class="layout">
      <ManuscriptPdfViewer :files="manuscript.manuscriptFiles" :activeFile="activeFile" />

      <ManuscriptSidebar
        :authors="authors"
        :files="manuscript.manuscriptFiles"
        @select-file="activeFile = $event"
      />
    </div>
  </div>
</template>

<style scoped>
.layout {
  margin-top: 16px;
  display: flex;
  gap: 32px;
}

=== FILE: \src\views\MainView.vue ===
<script setup>
import { ref, computed, onMounted } from 'vue'
import axios from 'axios'
import VueFeather from 'vue-feather'

const releases = ref([])
const volumes = ref([])
const manuscripts = ref([])
const authors = ref([])

/* --- Р·Р°РіСЂСѓР·РєР° РґР°РЅРЅС‹С… --- */
onMounted(async () => {
  const [releasesRes, volumesRes, manuscriptsRes, authorsRes] = await Promise.all([
    axios.get('http://localhost:3001/releases'),
    axios.get('http://localhost:3001/volumes'),
    axios.get('http://localhost:3001/manuscripts'),
    axios.get('http://localhost:3001/authors'),
  ])

  releases.value = releasesRes.data
  volumes.value = volumesRes.data
  manuscripts.value = manuscriptsRes.data
  authors.value = authorsRes.data
})
/* --- С‚РµРєСѓС‰РёР№ РІС‹РїСѓСЃРє --- */
const currentRelease = computed(() => {
  if (!releases.value.length) return null
  return releases.value[releases.value.length - 1]
})

/* --- С‚РµРєСѓС‰РёР№ С‚РѕРј --- */
const currentVolume = computed(() => {
  if (!currentRelease.value) return null

  const volId = currentRelease.value.volumes[0]
  return volumes.value.find((v) => v.volId === volId)
})

/* --- СЂСѓРєРѕРїРёСЃРё С‚РµРєСѓС‰РµРіРѕ С‚РѕРјР° --- */
const volumeManuscripts = computed(() => {
  if (!currentVolume.value) return []

  return currentVolume.value.manuscripts
    .map((id) => manuscripts.value.find((m) => m.manuscriptId === id))
    .filter(Boolean)
})

/* --- РіСЂСѓРїРїРёСЂРѕРІРєР° --- */
const grouped = computed(() => {
  const g = {}

  volumeManuscripts.value.forEach((m) => {
    if (!g[m.chapter]) g[m.chapter] = []
    g[m.chapter].push(m)
  })

  return g
})

/* --- Р°РІС‚РѕСЂС‹ --- */
const getAuthors = (ids) => {
  return ids
    .map((id) => authors.value.find((a) => a.authorsId === id)?.name)
    .filter(Boolean)
    .join(', ')
}

/* --- СЂР°Р·РґРµР»С‹ С‚РµРєСѓС‰РµРіРѕ С‚РѕРјР° --- */
const volumeChapters = computed(() => {
  return [...new Set(volumeManuscripts.value.map((m) => m.chapter))]
})
</script>

<template>
  <div v-if="currentRelease && currentVolume">
    <!-- Р’Р«РџРЈРЎРљ -->
    <div class="release-header">
      <img src="/РћР±Р»РѕР¶РєР°.jpg" class="release-cover" />

      <div class="text-blocks">
        <div class="title Lora-family large">РўРµРєСѓС‰РёР№ РІС‹РїСѓСЃРє в„–{{ currentRelease.relNum }}</div>

        <div class="title Lora-family large">
          {{ currentRelease.year }} | РўРѕРј
          {{ currentRelease.volumes.indexOf(currentVolume.volId) + 1 }}
        </div>

        <div class="title Lora-family big">Р Р°Р·РґРµР»С‹ РІ СЌС‚РѕРј С‚РѕРјРµ:</div>
        <span v-for="(chapter, index) in volumeChapters" :key="chapter" class="section-text">
          {{ chapter }}
          <span v-if="index !== volumeChapters.length - 1">, </span>
        </span>

        <button class="bright-button main" style="width: fit-content">
          РџРѕСЃРјРѕС‚СЂРµС‚СЊ РІСЃРµ С‚РѕРјР° Рё РІС‹РїСѓСЃРєРё <vue-feather type="arrow-right" size="20" />
        </button>
      </div>
    </div>

    <!-- РЎРўРђРўР¬Р -->
    <div v-for="(group, chapter) in grouped" :key="chapter">
      <div class="text-blocks">
        <div class="title Lora-family large">{{ chapter }}</div>

        <div v-for="(m, i) in group" :key="m.id" style="margin: 32px">
          <div>
            {{ i + 1 }}.
            <router-link
              :to="`/manuscripts/view/${m.manuscriptId}`"
              class="hyper-links main manuscripts"
            >
              {{ m.title }}
            </router-link>
          </div>

          <div class="section-text">
            {{ getAuthors(m.authorsId) }}
          </div>

          <div style="margin-top: 8px; padding-bottom: 8px">
            <button class="general-button main">
              РЎРєР°С‡Р°С‚СЊ <vue-feather type="download" size="18" />
            </button>
            <button class="general-button main">
              РЎРѕС…СЂР°РЅРёС‚СЊ <vue-feather type="bookmark" size="18" />
            </button>
          </div>
          <hr style="margin-left: -32px; color: var(--soft-purple)" />
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  </div>

  <div v-else>Р—Р°РіСЂСѓР·РєР°...</div>
</template>

<style scoped>

<script setup>
import { ref, onMounted } from 'vue'
import { useRoute } from 'vue-router'
import axios from 'axios'

import ManuscriptDetailsContainer from '@/components/editor/manuscriptDetails/ManuscriptDetailsContainer.vue'

const route = useRoute()

const manuscript = ref(null)
const authors = ref([])
const redactors = ref([])
const reviews = ref([])
const reviewers = ref([])

onMounted(async () => {
  const [manuscriptsRes, authorsRes, redactorsRes, reviewsRes, reviewersRes] = await Promise.all([
    axios.get('http://localhost:3001/manuscripts'),
    axios.get('http://localhost:3001/authors'),
    axios.get('http://localhost:3001/redactors'),
    axios.get('http://localhost:3001/reviews'),
    axios.get('http://localhost:3001/reviewers'),
  ])

  const id = route.params.id

  manuscript.value = manuscriptsRes.data.find((m) => m.id === id)

  authors.value = authorsRes.data
  redactors.value = redactorsRes.data
  reviews.value = reviewsRes.data
  reviewers.value = reviewersRes.data
})
</script>

<template>
  <div v-if="manuscript" style="padding: var(--spacing-sm) var(--spacing-lg)">
    <div
      class="text-blocks"
      style="
        padding-bottom: var(--spacing-lg);
        border-bottom: var(--strock-sm) solid var(--red-editor);
      "
    >
      <!-- РџРµСЂРІР°СЏ СЃС‚СЂРѕРєР° -->
      <div class="title Lora-family big">{{ manuscript.stage }}</div>

      <!-- РќР°Р·РІР°РЅРёРµ -->
      <div class="title Lora-family big">
        {{ manuscript.title }}
      </div>

      <!-- Р’РµСЂСЃРёСЏ Рё РґР°С‚Р° -->
      <div class="details-version-date">
        <span class="version-badge"> Р’РµСЂСЃРёСЏ {{ manuscript.version }} </span>
        <span class="section-text"> Р СѓРєРѕРїРёСЃСЊ РїРѕРґР°РЅР°: {{ manuscript.sentDate }}</span>
      </div>
    </div>
    <ManuscriptDetailsContainer
      :manuscript="manuscript"
      :authors="authors"
      :redactors="redactors"
      :reviews="reviews"
      :reviewers="reviewers"
    />
  </div>
</template>

=== FILE: \src\views\ManuscriptsView.vue ===
<script setup>
import { ref, onMounted, computed } from 'vue'
import axios from 'axios'

/* ======================
  Р”РѕР±Р°РІР»РµРЅРёРµ СЂСѓРєРѕРїРёСЃРµР№
======================= */
import AddManuscriptButton from '@/components/editor/manuscripts/AddManuscriptButton.vue'
import AddManuscriptModal from '@/components/editor/manuscripts/AddManuscriptModal.vue'
import AddManuscriptForm from '@/components/editor/manuscripts/AddManuscriptForm.vue'

import ManuscriptsSearchBar from '@/components/editor/manuscripts/ManuscriptsSearchBar.vue'
import ManuscriptsFilters from '@/components/editor/manuscripts/ManuscriptFilters.vue'
import ManuscriptsContainer from '@/components/editor/manuscripts/ManuscriptsContainer.vue'

const manuscripts = ref([])
const authors = ref([])
const redactors = ref([])

const searchQuery = ref('')

const statusFilter = ref('all')

const editorFilter = ref({
  mode: 'all',
  editors: [],
})

const showModal = ref(false)

onMounted(async () => {
  const [manuscriptsRes, authorsRes, redactorsRes] = await Promise.all([
    axios.get('http://localhost:3001/manuscripts'),
    axios.get('http://localhost:3001/authors'),
    axios.get('http://localhost:3001/redactors'),
  ])

  manuscripts.value = manuscriptsRes.data
  authors.value = authorsRes.data
  redactors.value = redactorsRes.data
})

/* =========================
   Р¤РёР»СЊС‚СЂР°С†РёСЏ СЂСѓРєРѕРїРёСЃРµР№
========================= */

const filteredManuscripts = computed(() => {
  let result = manuscripts.value

  /* =====================
     РџРћРРЎРљ
  ===================== */

  if (searchQuery.value) {
    const query = searchQuery.value.toLowerCase()

    result = result.filter((m) => {
      // РїРѕРёСЃРє РїРѕ id
      const idMatch = String(m.manuscriptId).includes(query)

      // РїРѕРёСЃРє РїРѕ РЅР°Р·РІР°РЅРёСЋ
      const titleMatch = m.title.toLowerCase().includes(query)

      // РїРѕРёСЃРє РїРѕ Р°РІС‚РѕСЂР°Рј
      const authorsMatch = m.authorsId?.some((authorId) => {
        const author = authors.value.find((a) => a.authorsId === authorId)
        return author?.name.toLowerCase().includes(query)
      })

      return idMatch || titleMatch || authorsMatch
    })
  }

  /* =====================
     Р¤РР›Р¬РўР  РЎРўРђРўРЈРЎРђ
  ===================== */

  if (statusFilter.value !== 'all') {
    result = result.filter((m) => m.stage === statusFilter.value)
  }

  /* =====================
     Р¤РР›Р¬РўР  Р Р•Р”РђРљРўРћР Рђ
  ===================== */

  const mode = editorFilter.value.mode

  if (mode === 'none') {
    result = result.filter((m) => !m.redactorsId || m.redactorsId.length === 0)
  }

  if (mode === 'mine') {
    const currentEditorId = 1

    result = result.filter((m) => m.redactorsId?.includes(currentEditorId))
  }

  if (mode === 'by_name') {
    result = result.filter((m) =>
      m.redactorsId?.some((id) => editorFilter.value.editors.includes(id)),
    )
  }

  return result
})
</script>

<template>
  <div class="manuscripts-page">
    <AddManuscriptButton @click="showModal = true" />

    <AddManuscriptModal v-if="showModal" @close="showModal = false">
      <AddManuscriptForm
        :authors="authors"
        :redactors="redactors"
        @saved="
          () => {
            showModal = false
            reloadManuscripts()
          }
        "
      />
    </AddManuscriptModal>

    <ManuscriptsSearchBar @search="searchQuery = $event" />

    <ManuscriptsFilters
      :status="statusFilter"
      :editorFilter="editorFilter"
      :editors="redactors"
      @change-status="statusFilter = $event"
      @change-editor-filter="editorFilter = $event"
    />

    <ManuscriptsContainer
      :manuscripts="filteredManuscripts"
      :authors="authors"
      :redactors="redactors"
    />
  </div>
</template>

<style scoped>
.manuscripts-page {
  display: flex;
  flex-direction: column;
  gap: 12px;
}
</style>

<script setup>
import { ref, onMounted } from 'vue'
import { useRoute } from 'vue-router'
import axios from 'axios'

import TaskDetailsActionsBar from '@/components/editor/taskDetails/TaskDetailsActionsBar.vue'
import TaskDetailsContainer from '@/components/editor/taskDetails/TaskDetailsContainer.vue'

const route = useRoute()
/*const taskId = Number(route.params.id)*/

const task = ref(null)
const manuscript = ref(null)
const authors = ref([])
const reviewers = ref([])
const reviews = ref([])
const users = ref([])

const activeTab = ref('overview')

onMounted(async () => {
  const [tasksRes, manuscriptsRes, authorsRes, reviewersRes, reviewsRes, usersRes] =
    await Promise.all([
      axios.get('http://localhost:3001/tasks'),
      axios.get('http://localhost:3001/manuscripts'),
      axios.get('http://localhost:3001/authors'),
      axios.get('http://localhost:3001/reviewers'),
      axios.get('http://localhost:3001/reviews'),
      axios.get('http://localhost:3001/users'),
    ])

  const taskId = route.params.id

  task.value = tasksRes.data.find((t) => String(t.id) === String(taskId))

  if (task.value) {
    manuscript.value = manuscriptsRes.data.find(
      (m) => Number(m.manuscriptId) === Number(task.value.manuscriptId),
    )
  }

  authors.value = authorsRes.data
  reviewers.value = reviewersRes.data
  reviews.value = reviewsRes.data
  users.value = usersRes.data
})

const setTab = (tab) => {
  activeTab.value = tab
}
</script>

<template>
  <div v-if="task && manuscript" style="padding: var(--spacing-sm) var(--spacing-lg)">
    <div
      class="text-blocks"
      style="
        padding-bottom: var(--spacing-lg);
        border-bottom: var(--strock-sm) solid var(--red-editor);
      "
    >
      <!-- РџРµСЂРІР°СЏ СЃС‚СЂРѕРєР° -->
      <div class="title Lora-family big">
        <span
          >{{ task.type }} &nbsp;|&nbsp; {{ task.stage }} &nbsp;|&nbsp; Р—Р°РґР°С‡Р° СЃРѕР·РґР°РЅР°:
          {{ task.createdAt }}</span
        >
      </div>

      <!-- РќР°Р·РІР°РЅРёРµ -->
      <div class="title Lora-family big">
        {{ manuscript.title }}
      </div>

      <!-- Р’РµСЂСЃРёСЏ Рё РґР°С‚Р° -->
      <div class="details-version-date">
        <span class="version-badge"> Р’РµСЂСЃРёСЏ {{ manuscript.version }} </span>
        <span class="section-text"> Р СѓРєРѕРїРёСЃСЊ РїРѕРґР°РЅР°: {{ manuscript.sentDate }} </span>
      </div>
    </div>

    <TaskDetailsActionsBar :activeTab="activeTab" @change-tab="setTab" />

    <TaskDetailsContainer
      :activeTab="activeTab"
      :task="task"
      :manuscript="manuscript"
      :authors="authors"
      :reviewers="reviewers"
      :reviews="reviews"
      :users="users"
    />
  </div>
</template>

=== FILE: \src\views\TasksView.vue ===
<script setup>
import { ref, onMounted, computed } from 'vue'
import axios from 'axios'

import TasksActionsBar from '@/components/editor/tasks/TasksActionsBar.vue'
import TasksContainer from '@/components/editor/tasks/TasksContainer.vue'

const tasks = ref([])
const manuscripts = ref([])
const authors = ref([])
const reviewers = ref([])
const reviews = ref([])

const activeFilter = ref('all')

onMounted(async () => {
  const [tasksRes, manuscriptsRes, authorsRes, reviewersRes, reviewsRes] = await Promise.all([
    axios.get('http://localhost:3001/tasks'),
    axios.get('http://localhost:3001/manuscripts'),
    axios.get('http://localhost:3001/authors'),
    axios.get('http://localhost:3001/reviewers'),
    axios.get('http://localhost:3001/reviews'),
  ])

  tasks.value = tasksRes.data
  manuscripts.value = manuscriptsRes.data
  authors.value = authorsRes.data
  reviewers.value = reviewersRes.data
  reviews.value = reviewsRes.data
})
const sortOrder = ref('new') // new | old

const filteredTasks = computed(() => {
  let result = tasks.value

  // Р¤РёР»СЊС‚СЂ РїРѕ С‚РёРїСѓ
  if (activeFilter.value !== 'all') {
    result = result.filter((task) => task.type === activeFilter.value)
  }

  // РЎРѕСЂС‚РёСЂРѕРІРєР° РїРѕ РґР°С‚Рµ СЃРѕР·РґР°РЅРёСЏ
  result = [...result].sort((a, b) => {
    const dateA = new Date(a.createdAt)
    const dateB = new Date(b.createdAt)

    return sortOrder.value === 'new' ? dateB - dateA : dateA - dateB
  })

  return result
})

const setFilter = (filter) => {
  activeFilter.value = filter
}

const setSortOrder = (order) => {
  sortOrder.value = order
}
</script>

<template>
  <div>
    <TasksActionsBar
      :activeFilter="activeFilter"
      :sortOrder="sortOrder"
      @change-filter="setFilter"
      @change-sort="setSortOrder"
    />

    <TasksContainer
      :tasks="filteredTasks"
      :manuscripts="manuscripts"
      :authors="authors"
      :reviewers="reviewers"
      :reviews="reviews"
    />
  </div>
</template>
